    var userstatus = "0";
    /* User drop down after login */
    // Close the dropdown if the user clicks outside of it
    window.onclick = function (event) {
        if (!event.target.matches('.dropbtn')) {

            var dropdowns = document.getElementsByClassName("dropdown-content");
            var i;
            for (i = 0; i < dropdowns.length; i++) {
                var openDropdown = dropdowns[i];
                if (openDropdown.classList.contains('show')) {
                    openDropdown.classList.remove('show');
                }
            }
        }
    };
    /* When the user clicks on the button, 
     toggle between hiding and showing the dropdown content */
    function dropdownMenu() {
        $("#myDropdown").toggle("show");
    }
</script>

    function slideNext(){
        var itemcount = 0;
        var count = $("#hiddencity").val();
        var nextcount = $("#nxtSlideCount").val();
        if(nextcount == 0){
            var totalCount = Number(count)+Number(count);
            var count1= count;
        }else{
            var totalCount = Number(nextcount)+Number(count);
            var count1= nextcount;
        } 
        if(itemcount > count1){ 
            $(".hidden-city").hide();
            $("#nxtSlideCount").val(totalCount);
            $("#prvSlideCount").val(count1);
            for(var i=Number(count1)+1;i<=totalCount;i++){
                $('#item'+i).show();
            }
        }
    }
    function prevSlide(){
        var itemcount = 0;
        var count = $("#hiddencity").val();
        var nextcount = $("#nxtSlideCount").val();
        var prevcount = $("#prvSlideCount").val();
        if(prevcount > 0){
            var totalCount = Number(nextcount)-Number(count);
            var totalCount1 = Number(prevcount)-Number(count);
            var count1= prevcount;

            $(".hidden-city").hide();
            $("#nxtSlideCount").val(totalCount);
            $("#prvSlideCount").val(totalCount1);
            for(var i=Number(totalCount1)+1;i<=count1;i++){
                $('#item'+i).show();
            }
        } 
    }

     function slideNextAlphabets(){
